850 entries, ghostty-control to gis-workflows. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **ghostty-control**: Script-first control for Ghostty on macOS. Open named terminals, run commands, drive TUIs, wait for rendered output, capture text or screenshots, manage splits/tabs, and clean up through composable gt-* scripts. Use when a task needs a real rendered terminal or terminal workspace. ([NishantJoshi00/ghostty-control](https://github.com/NishantJoshi00/ghostty-control/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★1)
- **ghostty-ctl**: 用类 tmux 的方式从命令行控制 macOS 上的 Ghostty 终端(&gt;=1.3)。能列出窗口/tab(含 name/index/cwd/pid/tty)、设置 tab 标题与终端前景/背景/光标/调色板颜色、向指定 tab 注入输入(粘贴/按键/执行命令)、读取某个 tab 的屏幕或回滚内容、执行任意 Ghostty 内置 action、以及新建/分屏/选择/关闭窗口与 tab。当用户想"脚本化操作 Ghostty 的 tab"、"给某个 tab 设标题/颜色"、"往某个终端发命令"、"抓某个 tab 的输出/日志"、"自动搭建终端布局"时使用。仅限 macOS + Ghostty。 ([hysios/ghostty-ctl](https://github.com/hysios/ghostty-ctl/tree/HEAD/plugins/ghostty-ctl/skills/ghostty-ctl))

USE THIS SKILL WHEN the user asks about Ghostty config, keybindings, themes, terminal escape sequences, or installation.
